./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:13:- 2026-06-12: AMB-669 / PLOS-041 defines released public Source Atlas pack paths as immutable content-bound addresses. ./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:20:- 2026-06-12: AMB-663 / PLOS-032 defines signing trust states as active, rotating, deprecated, revoked, or unknown. Unknown or compromised trust fails closed; revoked keys block production eligibility; rollback and last-known-good fallback require exact artifact ids, hashes, signer ids, release receipts, and non-revoked verification. This is policy/control-plane foundation only, not key infrastructure, signer trust model implementation, R2 publication, release recovery proof, or security certification.
